        "wiki.gg logo\n\nToggle personal tools menu\n\n* \n* \n\n[](https://app.wiki.gg/showcase/go?utm_campaign=conjurers-1768001949723&utm_medium=bannerheader&utm_source=warcraft.wiki.gg&location=desktop)\n\n# Console variables\n\nFrom Warcraft Wiki\n\nJump to navigation Jump to search\n\n_For World of Warcraft Classic cvars, see Console variables/Classic ._\n\n\u2191 _Widget API_\n\n|Main Menu |\n| --- |\n|* WoW API\n* Lua API\n* FrameXML API\n\n* * *\n\n* ScriptObject API\n* Widget scripts\n* XML schema\n* Events\n* CVars\n\n* * *\n\n* Macro commands\n* Combat Log\n* Escape sequences\n* Hyperlinks\n* API changes\n* HOWTOs\n* [Discord logo.png](https://discord.gg/txUg39Vhc6) [wowuidev](https://discord.gg/txUg39Vhc6) |\n\n\u2192 UI Tech. Details\n\nThe _World of Warcraft_ game client stores all of its configurations in console variables ( **CVars** ). These variables affect many aspects of the game such as the graphics, the sound system and the interface.\n\nThe value of a CVar can be queried with GetCVar . The complete list of CVars is available from C\\_Console.GetAllCommands .\n\n## Contents\n\n* 1 Setting CVars\n  \n    + 1\\.1 SetCVar\n    + 1\\.2 Console\n    + 1\\.3 Config.wtf\n* 2 CVar Scope\n* 3 Resetting CVars\n* 4 List of Console Variables\n* 5 List of Console Commands\n\n## Setting CVars\n\nThere are several ways of setting the console variables. The most commonly used CVars are tied to the Interface window settings.\n\n### SetCVar\n\nAddOns and scripts can set CVars by calling SetCVar .\n\n```\n/ run SetCVar ( \"scriptErrors\" , 1 )\n```\n\nNote\n\n**Note:** Secure CVars \ud83d\udee1\ufe0f cannot be set in combat and only with `SetCVar` instead of with `/console` .\n\n### Console\n\nWith the `/console` slash command.\n\n```\n/console scriptErrors 1\n```\n\nThe `/console enable` command or launching the game with the `WoW.exe -console` argument will allow you to open the Console window in-game by pressing the ````` or `~` key.\n\nConsole variables 01.png\n\n### Config.wtf\n\nThe Config.wtf file stores settings mostly relevant to game startup, such as `gxMaximize` for the screen window size and `locale` for the language.\n\n```\nWorld of Warcraft\\_retail_\\WTF\\Config.wtf\n```\n\nThe config-cache.wtf files are used for character and WoW account-specific settings. They are synchronized to the server if the synchronizeConfig CVar is enabled (by default).\n\n```\nWorld of Warcraft\\_retail_\\WTF\\Account\\ <AccountName> \\ <Server> \\ <Character> \\config-cache.wtf\nWorld of Warcraft\\_retail_\\WTF\\Account\\ <AccountName> \\config-cache.wtf\n```\n\nConfig files can be manually edited while the game is not running; they would otherwise be overwritten on reloading or logging out.\n\n## CVar Scope\n\nSome CVars are character-specific or WoW account-wide, for example:\n\n* When setting nameplateMotion , it will only be changed for your current character.\n* When setting chatBubbles , it will not be changed for any other WoW accounts on your Blizzard account.\n\n## Resetting CVars\n\nUse GetCVarDefault or the cvar\\_default command to reset a CVar, for example:\n\n```\n/ run SetCVar ( \"autoSelfCast\" , GetCVarDefault ( \"autoSelfCast\" ))\n```\n\n```\n/console cvar_default autoSelfCast\n```\n\nThe [AdvancedInterfaceOptions](https://www.curseforge.com/wow/addons/advancedinterfaceoptions) addon can show which cvars were changed from the default:\n\nAdvancedInterfaceOptions cvar browser\n\nTo reset the whole user interface:\n\n```\n/console cvar_default\n```\n\n    Red exclamation mark icon Changing a character or account-specific CVar cannot be undone or reverted by uninstalling, repairing the game or [deleting](https://us.battle.net/support/en/article/7549) the WTF folder as they will be synchronized from the server again.\n\n## List of Console Variables\n\nNote\n\n**Note:**
